Key algorithm details:

`fast_convolution(a)`: use real FFT, next power of two `N = 1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()`. Could optimize by precomputing N and using `np.fft.rfft` each iter. Could use `np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)`. Ensure c small negatives zero.

`fast_score(a)`: if len n; sum; c; max; `2*n*max / sum^2`. If n small. Use `np.max(c)`. For speed, use `c = fast_convolution`; clip.

`normalize(a, target_sum)`: as previous, clip 0..1000, sum target. If max*target/sum >1000? Our sequences mean 1, max maybe <1000. Keep simple.

Starts:
- `make_gaussian(n, sigma, center=0.5)` where sigma in units of length. Use `x = np.linspace(0,1,n)`, `a=exp(-0.5*((x-center)/sigma)**2)`, normalize. sigma from `sigma*(n-1)`. If sigma≥0.1.
- `make_double_gaussian(n, d, sigma, weight?)`: centers 0.5±d, sum two exponentials.
- `make_cosine_window(n, mode, amp)`: `1 + amp*cos(2π*mode*x+phase)` exponentiate? Ensure positive. For mode 1 with phase 0 gives edge hump; phase π center hump. Use `exp(amp*cos...)` maybe.
- `make_polynomial(n, beta)`: `1 - (2x-1)^2` clipped? `a = np.maximum(0, 1 - alpha*(2x-1)^2)` with alpha in [-?]. If alpha negative center negative? Better use `a=np.exp(beta*(1 - (2x-1)^2))` shaped.
- `make_asym_gaussian(n, center, sigma)`.
- Parent upsample.
- Random smooth (harmonic) with few frequencies.
- Maybe upsample height_sequence_1 and its centroids.

We can precompute starts for a length and score. Select top k to refine.

Soft-power implementation details:
```
def soft_power_search(a0, budget_s, seed=0, base_p=8.0, use_line_search=True):
    n=len(a0); target=n
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    a=positive_normalize(a0, target)
    c=fast_convolution(a); s=a.sum(); score=fast_score_array(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    eta=0.1; beta_p=base_p; recurrent...
    it=0
    while time<deadline:
       c=fast conv (we have)
       M=max(c)
       if M<=0 break
       z=c/M
       # weights: z^p
       # To avoid overflow/underflow and ensure sum:
       # For p close large, log weights = p*log(z+eps)
       # Use z^p if p<=32; log if p>...
       if p<50:
          w = z**p
          s_w = w.sum()
       else:
          logw = p * np.log(np.maximum(z, 1e-300))
          logw -= np.max(logw)
          w = np.exp(logw)
          s_w = w.sum()
       w /= s_w
       grad = weighted_gradient(a,w) # length n
       g = grad - grad.mean()
       denom = np.linalg.norm(g) or max abs
       if denom<1e-12: break
       g /= denom
       # maybe decay noise
       logf = -eta*g + rng.normal(0, noise, n)
       logf = np.clip(logf, -3, 3)
       cand = a * np.exp(logf)
       # floor small: cand = np.where(cand>eps, cand, eps)
       cand = normalize(cand, target)
       c2=fast conv; score2; soft_score2 maybe sum(c2/(maxc2/M?))^p? Hard.
       if score2 < best_score: best...
       # acceptance: if exact score2 <= score*(1+tol) or soft improves?
```
Need a scalar soft objective to guide acceptance: `soft = np.sum((c/mc)^p)` with mc maybe current max. But if mc changes, not monotonic. Use `soft = logsumexp(log(c))`? Simpler accept if `score2 < best_score` update best; accept current if `score2 <= score*(1+accept_rel)`. This may not improve soft if exact flat. Use small accept_rel. Or compute `soft = np.sum(c**p)` scaled by M^p: `soft = np.sum((c/M)^p)`. At new cand, use its M. If soft2 < soft (or exact close), accept. Since M not fixed, but okay as progress heuristic.

But line search with multiple eta values: For each tried eta, compute candidate and score. To save, evaluate one. Use backtracking: if exact score not decreased (and soft not), reduce eta. If improved, increase. But we want minimize exact final. Use `score2` exact. In nonconvex, soft may decrease but exact not; still update current if within tolerance. Simulated annealing: accept if score2 <= score*(1+tol). Increase temperature? Use noise.

Parameter p: We can run multiple independent local searches at same length with different p: p=2 (quadratic), p=4, p=8, p=16, p=32, p=inf topK. The best across them. For n large, topK previous. Use time split among p values.

Maybe better: Anneal p within one search: start p low to shape broad, then increase p to minimize max. E.g. p schedule: 2,4,8,16,32,64. When p increases, weights more peak. This can converge to min-max. Implement `anneal_p` by increasing every certain iterations or when stagnant. But if p low, objective not max; could move away from exact best. At the end, high p. Could just run separate searches.

Weighted gradient function:
```
def weighted_gradient(a, w):
    # w length ≥2n-1
    n=a.size
    if n==0...
    N = next_power_of_two(max(2*n-1, w.size)) # same length
    ra = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    rw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(ra*rw, N)
    return 2*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need ensure w length exactly L=2n-1. If c length L. Good. This computes ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j w_{i+j}. Test for n=2: a=[a0,a1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. i=0: sum_j a_j w_j = a0w0+a1w1. conv(ar,w) ar=[a1,a0]. conv indices: m=1: a1w0 + a0w1? Wait formula m=i+n-1. For i=0, m=1. conv[1]= ar0 w1 + ar1 w0 = a1w1 + a0w0 good. i=1, m=2: ar0w2+ar1w1 = a1w2 + a0w1. Good. Gradient 2. For k derivative: For i=0, contributions from k where j=k: w_k a_{k}; first row k=0 w0 a0, k=1 w1 a1 => 2(a0w0+a1w1) matches derivation. Good.

Can optimize by using `N` same as convolution. Store `fft_cache`? Not across calls due a change. Could compute c and grad simultaneously: `fa = rfft(a,N); ra = rfft(a[::-1],N)`. For c use fa*fa. For grad need rfft(w). So per iter: rfft(a), rfft(a[::-1]), rfft(w), inverse conv. That's 4 FFTs. Could avoid rfft(a) for grad? weighted conv uses rev a. Need fa for c. Could compute c via convolution and grad via one? If w derived from c, need both. 4 FFT length N. For n=8192 N=32768, 4*32768 complex FFT maybe ~0.02s? 50 it/s. For 2400s, 120k iterations. Enough.

Can compute c from `irfft(fa*fa)`. If we already have `ar = a[::-1]`, `ra = rfft(ar)`. `fa` maybe not needed if we compute c via same `reverse`? `a*a` convolution not same as `rev(a)*?`. Need fa. Could use `np.fft.rfft(a, N)`.

Alternative: derivative for symmetric weights maybe can be computed via `np.convolve`? FFT fine.

Soft objective evaluation could be integrated: after c, M, weights, `soft = (w * c/M).sum()`? Since w = normalized c^p, sum w c/M = sum c^{p+1}/(sum c^p M). Not same. But okay. Use `soft = np.sum((c/M)**p)` (unnormalized), with p same. For p=8, if many coefficients, maybe large but finite. To reduce boundary of zeros, maybe use p=4. Line search exact.

`local_search` can also use `linear_lp_step` when n≤LP_N. Implementing LP optional but could be powerful. Let's design robust LP step with time guard.

LP Step implementation:
```
def lp_blend_step(f, budget=??, t=0.2):
    n=f.size
    c=fast_convolution(f); M=max(c); S=sum(f)
    # Build A maybe dense; if memory risk return None
    L=2*n-1
    A = np.empty((L,n), dtype=float)
    # fill using stride: for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j] = f
    # This allocates 67MB for n=2048; okay. Could use float32? Use float64.
    for j in range(n): A[j:j+n, j] = f
    # To speed, for n>1024 use sparse? But dense okay for up to 1536 maybe. Use chunk if MemoryError.
    try:
       from scipy.optimize import linprog
       res = linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(L, M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs')
    except: return None
    if res.status !=0: return None
    g = res.x
    # sanitize
    g = np.clip(g,0,1000); Sg=sum(g); if Sg<1e-12 return None
    g = normalize(g, S) # rescale to same sum
    blend = (1-t)*f + t*g; normalize
    return blend
```
But dense A construction with `A[j:j+n,j]=f` is not efficient because assigning full vector each j copies f every row? It writes n^2 values. For n=2048, 4M writes? Actually n columns * n length =4M?     a=normalize(a0,n)
```
    best=a; best_score=score
    c=fast_conv(a); score
    eta=0.15; eta_min=0.005; eta_max=1.0
    beta? p = p_start; p_inc = log(p_end/p_start)/(budget/0.5?) maybe every far
    noise=0.03; accept_rel=0.02
    it=0; stagnant=0
    while time<deadline:
       # maybe increase p with iteration: p = p_start * (p_end/p_start)**min(1, it/500)
       compute weights from c with current p
       grad...
       if gradient norm small: perturb
       trial...
       accept if exact score improves or soft improves and exact within tol?
       if no improve for 200 iters:
           if allow_lp: try lp_blend a from best, accept if score<best
           if still stagnant: smooth_perturb best with larger noise; reset p maybe lower, eta=0.2
       if improved: eta*=1.05; noise*=0.99; stagnant=0
       else: eta*=0.8
    return best,best
```

Let's design `lp_refine(f, budget, seed)`:
```
f=normalize(f,n)
best=f; best_score=score
t=0.2
while time<deadline:
    g = solve_lp_direction(f) # returns g normalized sunder same sum or None
    if g None: break
    # line search over t values maybe
    improved=False
    for tt in [t, 0.5*t, 0.25*t, 0.1, 0.05]:
       cand=normalize((1-tt)*best + tt*g, n)
       sc=fast_score(cand)
       if sc < best_score*(1-1e-12): best=cand; best_score=sc; improved=True; break
    if improved: t=min(0.5,t*1.1); if stagnant... else t=max(0.02,t*0.7)
    if not improved:
       # try next f=best still; maybe update f? if g based on best and no improvement due t too small? derivative says at t>0 small nonincrease; if no improvement try new g from best after smoothing? break after limit
```

```
def lp_minconv_direction(f, time_limit=20.0):
    n=len(f); L=2*n-1
    if n>MAX_LP_N: return None
    f = np.asarray(f); c=fast_convolution(f); M=float(np.max(c)); if not finite return None
    # ensure f positive? If zeros, LP still okay? A may have zero diagonals, but g sum constraint and f nonnegative. If some constraints all zero for row? If f all? no. If f_i=0, some constraints less restrictive but bounded by sum. No need floor. But to avoid degeneracy, f = max(f, 0.0).
    try:
      from scipy import sparse
      from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except: return None
    # Build A
    try:
       diag_data = np.repeat(f[:, None], n, axis=1)
       offsets = np.arange(n)
       A = sparse.dia_matrix((diag_data, offsets), shape=(L, n), dtype=np.float64).tocsr()
    except MemoryError: return None
    # Add -t column
    try:
       Acol = sparse.csr_matrix(-np.ones((L,1), dtype=np.float64))
       Afull = sparse.lstack([A, Acol]).tocsr()
    except: ...
    c = np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1
    Aeq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1
    C? c_obj
    bounds = [(0,None)]*n + [(0,M)]
    res = linprog(c, A_ub=Afull, b_ub=np.zeros(L), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[float(n)], bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':'high', 'time_limit': max(1,time_limit)})
    if res is None: return None
    if res.status not in (0,1) maybe accept if finite.
    x = np.asarray(res.x)
    g=x[:n]; t=x[-1]
    sanitize g: clip nonnegative; sum scale to n if close; if sum not n due solver, normalize? But changing g scaling increases t? We can rescale g to sum n and t *= n/sum? Since constraints linear: if g scaled down, t can scale down. If sum_g not n but positive, scale g to n: factor = n/sum. Then t = t*factor? Wait constraints A(g_scaled) <= t? If original A g <= t, scaling by α changes t required α t. If α<1 (scale down), α t ≤ t okay. But to get minimal for scaled, use α t. If α>1, may violate; but solver should sum n. We can if |sum-n| small adjust by small. Use factor and t*=factor if factor <=1, else not. Then g = normalize? Ensure g≤? no.
    if t > M: t=M; maybe rescale g to n with t? If g scaled down, okay.
    return g,t,M
```

```
def soft_power_search(a0, budget, seed=0, p_start=2.0, p_end=64.0, anneal_iters=800, stale_limit=400, allow_restarts=True):
    n=len(a0); target=float(n)
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    a=positive_normalize(a0,target, floor=target/(10*n)?)
    c=fast_convolution(a); score=fast_score_array(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    it=0; eta=0.12; noise=0.02; accept_rel=0.01
    p=p_start
    while time<deadline and stable:
       if it % something print
       c=fast convolution (reuse from previous? after candidate update c computed; if accept/cand we have c2. Use current c)
       M=max(c)
       # compute weights
       w = power_weights(c, p)
       grad=weighted_gradient(a,w)
       g=grad - grad.mean()
       scale = g.std()
       if scale < 1e-12: handle flat
       g /= scale
       # Try maybe line search with 3 eta values to improve robust
       best_eta=eta; best_cand=a; best_cscore=inf
       for try_eta in [eta*0.5, eta, eta*1.5]: # optional, costs 3 convs
           logf = -try_eta*g + rng.normal(0, noise, n)
           logf clip [-3,3]
           cand=normalize(a*exp(logf), target)
           cscore=fast_score(cand)
           if cscore < best... choose
       # But doing 3 per iter triples cost. Could do one with adaptive backtracking.
       cand_score = fast_score
       soft = ... maybe not.
       if cand_score < best_score*(1-1e-12): best...
       if cand_score <= score*(1+accept_rel): accept a=cand, c=..., score=cand_score; eta= min(1, eta*1.08); noise=max(0.002, noise*0.995); stagnant=0
       else: eta=max(0.01, eta*0.75); noise maybe; stagnant+=1
       # Increase p gradually:
       p = p_start*(p_end/p_start)**min(1, it/anneal_iters)
       # if stagnant>limit:
          # restart: a = smooth_perturb(best, strength=0.2..0.5); c=...; score=...; eta=0.25; noise=0.05; p maybe p_start*0.5? stagnant=0
          # if stagnation too long, break? Use time.
```

`lp_refine` details:
```
def lp_refine(f, budget_s, max_solves=100, seed=0):
   f=normalize(f,n)
   best=f; best_score=score
   t_blend=0.25
   stale=0
   t0=time
   solves=0
   while time < deadline and solves<max_solves:
      dir = lp_minconv_direction(best, time_limit=max(5, min(60, remaining*0.2)))
      if dir is None: break
      g, t_opt = dir
      # line search
      improved=False
      for blend in [t_blend, 0.5*t_blend, 0.25*t_blend, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02]:
          cand=(1-blend)*best + blend*g; normalize
          sc=fast_score(cand)
          if sc < best_score - 1e-12*best_score:
              best=cand; best_score=sc; improved=True; break
      if improved:
         t_blend=min(0.6, t_blend*1.15); stale=0
      else:
         t_blend=max(0.02, t_blend*0.7); stale+=1
         if stale>4: break
      solves+=1
      print progress maybe
   return best, best_score
```
But the LP direction depends on `best`; after accepting new best, next solve. If no improvement due numerical, maybe because g=best. We can check `np.max(np.abs(g-best))` small -> break. Also if t_opt >= best_score*M? Actually if t_opt close current M, no improvement.

Potential problem: LP min t may find g that minimizes f*g but g*g may be worse. The blend derivative guarantees first-order nonincrease if for all active peaks f*g_k ≤M. LP ensures f*g_k ≤ t_opt ≤ M. For small blend, exact score should not increase to first order. But second-order could increase. Line search small should find. If not, fallback soft.

```
def power_weights(c, p):
    m = c.max()
    if m <=0: return np.ones_like(c)/len
    z = c / m
    if p <= 1.5:
       # Could just uniform? p=1 weights uniform? We'll use p>=2.
    if p < 32:
       w = z ** p  # if z has zeros fine
       sw=w.sum()
       if sw <=0: w[np.argmax(c)] =1
       else: w /= sw
    else:
       # log to avoid underflow? z is <=1, z**p underflows for z<0.99. But we can compute log only for z>0.
       # Since z values many, p=64, z^64 may underflow for z<exp(-16/64)=0.78. That's okay, weights only near max. But if no weights? max z=1 remains.
       w = z ** p is okay in float64 underflows to0.
    return w
```

`smooth_perturb` for restarts:
```
def smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength):
   n=len(a)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=zeros
   for m in 1..4: amp=strength/(m+1); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π); z += amp*np.cos(2πmx+ph)
   a = a*np.exp(z)
   if n>16 and rng.random()<0.4:
      # local Gaussian bump multiplicative
      center=rng.random(); width=rng.uniform(0.02,0.2); amp=rng.normal(0, strength)
      z=np.exp(-0.5*((x-center)/width)**2)*amp
      a*=np.exp(z)
   a=max(a,eps); normalize target
   return a
```
This maintains smooth.

Let's detail functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _pair_score(pair): return pair[0]

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def normalize_sequence(a, target_sum, max_value=1000.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return a
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,max_value)
    for _ in range(4):
        s=float(a.sum())
        if s<=1e-15:
            a.fill(target_sum/a.size)
            return a
        a*=target_sum/s
        a=np.clip(a,0.0,max_value)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=1e-15: a.fill(target_sum/a.size)
    else: a*=target_sum/s
    return np.clip(a,0.0,max_value)
```

Maybe if target_sum/a.size when a.size=0. Check.

```
def power_weights(c,p):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    L=c.size
    if m<=1e-300:
        w=np.ones(L)/L; return w
    z=c/m
    if p < 2.0:
        # maybe uniform for p=1? Use p=2 min
        w=np.ones(L)/L; return w
    # If p large and z mostly <0.9, z**p underflow; okay. But if z has nan? no.
    with np.errstate(under='ignore', over='ignore'):
        w=z**p
    w=np.where(np.isfinite(w),w,0.0)
    sw=float(w.sum())
    amax=np.argmax(c)
    if sw<=0.0 or not math.isfinite(sw):
        w.fill(0.0); w[amax]=1.0
    else:
        w /= sw
    # Ensure max point nonzero
```

```
def _add_regular(starts, a, n):

   try:
      c=fast_convolution(a)
      s=float(a.sum())
      sc=score_from_sum(c,n,s)
   except: return
   if np.isfinite(sc): starts.append((sc,a))
```

Local search functions:

`soft_power_search(a0, budget_s, seed=0, p_start=2.0, p_end=64.0, N=None, target=None)`:
Detailed implementation:

```
def soft_power_search(a0, budget_s, seed=0, p_start=2.0, p_end=96.0, restart_limit? ):
    a0=np.asarray(a0)
    n=int(a0.size)
    if n<=0: return a0, inf
    target=float(n)
    if N is None: N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=positive_normalize(a0,target)
    c=fast_convolution(a,N)
    score=score_from_c(c,n,target)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    eta=0.12; eta_min=0.005; eta_max=1.2
    noise=0.015; accept_rel=0.02
    it=0; no_improve=0; restart_count=0
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(0.0,budget_s)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       if not finite a or sum<=0: a=best.copy(); c=fast_convolution(a,N); score=best_score
       # p schedule
       frac = min(1.0, it / 600.0)
       p = p_start * (p_end/p_start) ** frac
       M=float(np.max(c))
       if M <= 1e-300: break
       w=power_weights(c,p)
       grad=weighted_gradient_nt(a,w,N)
       g = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
       scale=float(np.std(g))
       if scale <=1e-12:
          # gradient flat or uniform; break/restart
          if no_improve > 100: break
          a=smooth_perturb(best,rng,0.3,target); c=...; score=...
          no_improve=0; continue
       g /= scale
       # maybe smooth gradient? no
       logf = -eta * g
       if noise>1e-6:
          logf += rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
       logf=np.clip(logf,-2.5,2.5)
       cand=a*np.exp(logf)
       cand=positive_normalize(cand,target)
       c2=fast_convolution(cand,N)
       sc2=score_from_c(c2,n,target)
       if not finite sc2:
           eta=max(eta_min, eta*0.7); continue
       if sc2 < best_score*(1.0-1e-12) - 1e-12:
           best=cand.copy(); best_score=sc2; no_improve=0
           eta=min(eta_max, eta*1.08)
           accept_rel=max(0.001, accept_rel*0.998)
           noise=max(0.002, noise*0.997)
       else:
           no_improve += 1
       if sc2 <= score*(1.0+accept_rel):
           a=cand; c=c2; score=sc2
           if sc2 < score? etc
           if sc2 < best_score? already.
       else:
           eta=max(eta_min, eta*0.75)
           # maybe do not accept; c remains current
       # stagnation
       if no_improve > 350:
           strength = 0.25 + 0.1*min(3,restart_count)
           cand=smooth_perturb(best,rng,strength,target)
           c=fast_convolution(cand,N); sc=score_from_c(c,n,target)
           # accept perturbed as current even if score worse, but ensure not too worse?
           if np.isfinite(sc) and (sc < best_score*1.1 or restart_count<3):
              a=cand; c=c2? score=sc
           else: a=best.copy(); c=fast...
           restart_count +=1; no_improve=0; eta=0.2; accept_rel=0.05; noise=0.05
           # reset p? We can set it? Instead keep p but maybe lower:
           # Can't easily reset frac. We can decrease p by setting `p_offset`? Simpler: if restart_count>2, continue.
       # print every 500 iterations
       if it % 500 ==0: print(...);
    return best,best_score
```

```
phase=0.0 # 0..1
while...:
   p=p_start*(p_end/p_start)**phase

   on restart: phase = max(0, phase-0.5) maybe.
```

`lp_minconv_direction(f, time_limit, max_n=2048)`:
```
def lp_minconv_direction(f, time_limit=20.0):
    n=len(f)
    if n>MAX_LP_N: return None
    if n<2: return None
    c=fast_convolution(f); M=float(np.max(c)); if M<=0 return None
    try from scipy import sparse, from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except: return None
    L=2*n-1
    # Build A
    use_dense = n <= 768
    if use_dense:
       Afull = np.zeros((L,n+1), dtype=np.float64)
       Afull[:, -1] = -1.0
       idx=np.arange(n)
       for d in range(n):
           Afull[d+idx, idx] = f[d]
       Aeq = np.zeros((1,n+1), dtype=np.float64); Aeq[0,:n]=1
       c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1
       bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n + [(0.0, M)]
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=Afull, b_ub=np.zeros(L), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[float(n)], bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':'high', 'time_limit': max(1.0,time_limit)})
    else:
       try:
          diag_data=np.repeat(f[:,None], n, axis=1)
          offsets=np.arange(n)
          A=sparse.dia_matrix((diag_data, offsets), shape=(L,n), dtype=np.float64).tocsr()
          tcol=sparse.csr_matrix(-np.ones((L,1), dtype=np.float64))
          Afull=sparse.lstack([A,_tcol]).tocsr()
          Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1),dtype=np.float64); Aeq[0,:n]=1
          c_obj=...; bounds=...
          res=linprog(...)
       except Exception: return None
    if res is None: return None
    # accept if status 0 or x finite and constraints okay
    if not hasattr(res,'x') or res.x is None: return None
    x=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
    if len(x)!=n+1: return None
    g=x[:n]; t=x[-1]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)) or not finite(t): return None
    g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
    sumg=float(g.sum())
    if sumg <=0 or abs(sumg - n) > max(1e-3*n,1e-6):
       # if close, normalize; else discard
       if sumg>0 and abs(sumg-n) <= 1e-2*n:
          factor=n/sumg; g*=factor; t*=factor
       else: return None
    # also t should be <=M+tol
    if t > M*(1+1e-5):
       # If due scaling, try clip? If t too large, solver didn't improve; discard or scale g down?
       if t > M*1.05: return None
       t=M
    # Ensure no negative; normalize tiny
    g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
    s2=float(g.sum())
    if s2 <=0: return None
    g = g * (float(n)/s2)
    # Optionally recompute actual t? Not necessary.
    return g, t
```
But if solver returns status not optimal, x may be infeasible. We can test `A @ g`? For dense we have Afull; for sparse maybe not. Could compute cross convolution via FFT to check max(f*g) and sum. This is just one FFT. Do it:
```
fg = cross_convolution(f,g); maxfg=float(np.max(fg)); sumg=...; if maxfg > M*(1+1e-5) or abs(sumg-n)>...: return None; t=max(t, maxfg) or t=maxfg
```
This ensures constraints and sum. If t > M, no descent; discard unless t≤M. If solver found t_opt <M, due tolerance maxfg maybe t+eps. Good. Use this check; cost one FFT. We'll implement `cross_convolution` with same N.

`cross_convolution(a,b,N=None)`: L = len(a)+len(b)-1. Use `np.fft.rfft(a,N)*rfft(b,N)`. If N None. Clip.

Then g feasible. Set `t_actual = max(fg)`. If `t_actual > M*(1+1e-6)`: maybe scale g down by `M/t_actual` if factor > 0.9; then sum changes; rescale to n? But scaling down and then up violates. If factor close 1, okay; if not, discard. Use if factor > 0.95: g *= factor; then sumg = sumg*factor; normalize? Actually we need sum n for descent; can't. If t_actual ≤ M, good. If t_actual slightly >M, we can scale g down and then add tiny uniform? Not worth; discard.

But if solver optimal t*<M, t_actual may be t*+1e-9; <=M. Good.

`lp_refine` line search: use g feasible t≤M. For blend values. The theoretical descent holds for active peaks because f*g≤t≤M. Use candidates exact score. If blend=0.5 maybe good.

```
def lp_refine(f, budget_s, seed=0, max_solves=200):
   n=len(f); target=n; N=...
   f=positive_normalize(f,target); c=fast...; best=...; score
   blend=0.25; stale=0; solves=0
   t0=...
   while time<deadline and solves<max_solves:
      remain=deadline-time
      if remain<3: break
      timelimit=min(20.0, max(2.0, remain*0.25))
      g,t = lp_minconv_direction(best, timelimit)
      if g is None: break
      if np.max(np.abs(g-best)) < 1e-5*target: break
      improved=False
      for b in [blend, 0.5*blend, 0.25*blend, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02]:
         if b<=0: continue
         cand=(1-b)*best + b*g
         cand=positive_normalize(cand,target)
         sc=fast_score_nt(cand,N) # need sum
         if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
             best=cand; best_score=sc; improved=True; break
      if improved:
         blend=min(0.6, blend*1.2); stale=0
         print...
      else:
         blend=max(0.02, blend*0.7); stale+=1
         if stale>5: break
      solves+=1
   return best,best_score
```

```
def local_search(a0,budget_s,seed=0,use_lp_n=2048,lp_frac=0.35):
   a0=normalize...
   n=len(a0)
   if budget_s<=0: return a0, score
   best_seq=a0; best_score=score
   lp_budget=0
   if n <= use_lp_n:
      lp_budget = min(budget_s*lp_frac, max(15.0, 400.0*512.0/n))
      lp_budget = min(lp_budget, budget_s - 10)
   if lp_budget>5:
      seq,sc=lp_refine(a0,lp_budget,seed)
      if sc<best_score: best_seq=seq; best_score=sc
   soft_budget=budget_s-time.monotonic()-t0
   if soft_budget>2:
      seq,sc=soft_power_search(best_seq, soft_budget, seed, p_start=2, p_end=128)
      if sc<best_score: ...
   return best_seq,best_score
```

Let's think about lower-level optimization in local search: Recomputing `power_weights(c,p)` with `z**p` over L=33k each iter; cheap. `weighted_gradient` does two FFTs and inverse. `fast_convolution` one rfft and inverse. Could combine c computation with weighted gradient? In each iter, we need c to compute weights, then grad. We already compute c previous iteration? At start, c current. After candidate accepted, we have c2. At next iter, c available. So no need separate fast conv for c; just use c. For candidate generation, we need cand c to evaluate score. After accept, set c = cand_c. If reject, keep old c. So per accepted iter: one convolution for candidate (cnt) + gradient FFTs (2 rfft+inverse). For rejected, also one candidate convolution + gradient = same. Per iter: 3 FFTs (candidate conv: rfft cand, irfft; gradient: rfft rev a, rfft w, irfft). Actually candidate conv needs rfft cand + inverse (2); gradient needs rfft rev a + rfft w + inverse (3). Total 5 FFTs. Plus if a changes after accept, rev a changes. `_next_pow2_size(n)` where n items length L.

```
def convolution_nt(a, N=None):
    n = a.size
    L = 2*n-1
    if N is None: N = _next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:L]
    np.maximum(out,0.0,out=out)
    return out
```
If n=0 return empty.

`cross_convolution_nt(a,b,N=None)`: L=a.size+b.size-1.

`score_from_c(c,n,s)`.

`fast_score_array(a)` uses sum.

`score_array_nt(a,N)`: sum.

```
return a
```
But if `floor` too large and max clip? no.

`sanitize_for_return`:
```
def sanitize_for_return(seq):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    if seq.size==0: return [1.0]*1024
    seq=np.where(np.isfinite(seq),seq,0.0)
    seq=np.clip(seq,0,1000)
    n=seq.size
    s=float(seq.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
       return [1.0]*1024
    scale=n/s
    if scale>0 and seq.max()*scale >1000:
       # clip after scale? maybe reduce scale to 1000/seq.max()
       scale=1000/max(seq.max(),1e-300)
       # after scaling sum may be <0.01? no
    seq=seq*scale
    seq=np.clip(seq,0,1000)
    # if sum <0.01, fallback
    if seq.sum()<0.01: return [1.0]*1024
    # If any nan no.
    out=[float(x) for x in seq]
    # ensure not bool
    return out
```

Now, functions details code:

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_sequence`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a, target_sum, max_value=1000.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = a.copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, max_value)
    target_sum = float(target_sum)
    for _ in range(4):
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            if a.size > 0:
                a.fill(target_sum / float(a.size))
            return a
        a *= target_sum / s
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, max_value)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        a.fill(target_sum / float(a.size))
    else:
        a *= target_sum / s
    a = np.clip(a,0.0,max_value)
    return a
```
If after clipping sum not target, loop handled? It may still not sum target if cap too low, but target small. Good.

`convolution_nt`:
```
def convolution_nt(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0: return np.empty(0,dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2*n - 1
    if N is None or N < L: N = _next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c
```
`cross_convolution_nt(a,b,N=None)` similar with L = a.size+b.size-1.

`score_from_c(c,n,s)`:
```
def score_from_c(c,n,s):
    if c.size==0: return inf
    s=float(s)
    if s < 0.01: return inf
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(mx): return inf
    return float(2.0*float(n)*mx/(s*s))
```

`fast_score_array(a)`:
```
def fast_score_array(a,N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a); n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    c=convolution_nt(a,N)
    return score_from_c(c,n,s)
```

`positive_normalize(a,target,floor=1e-10)`:
```
def positive_normalize(a,target,floor=1e-10):
    a=normalize_sequence(a,target)
    if a.size==0: return a
    floor=max(0.0,float(floor))
    if floor >0:
       a=np.where(a > floor, a, floor)
       a=normalize_sequence(a,target)
    return a
```
But after second normalize, some values may become below floor again? If floor large. not. Good.

`smoothing`:
```
def smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength, target):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return a
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    z=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if n>2:
       for m in range(1,5):
          amp=strength/(m+1.0)
          ph=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi)
          z += amp*np.cos(2.0*math.pi*m*x + ph)
    if n > 16:
       if rng.random() < 0.65:
          center=rng.uniform(0.0,1.0)
          width=rng.uniform(0.02,0.18)
          amp=rng.normal(0.0, strength)
          z += amp*np.exp(-0.5*((x-center)/width)**2)
    cand = a * np.exp(z)
    return positive_normalize(cand, target, 1e-10)
```
If z large, exp overflow? strength <=0.5, z few. Good.

`upsample_sequence`:
```
def upsample_sequence(seq,n):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    n=int(n)
    if n<=0: return seq
    if seq.size==0: return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n), float(n))
    if seq.size==n: return normalize_sequence(seq,float(n))
    xold=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,seq.size)
    xnew=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    out=np.interp(xnew,xold,seq)
    return normalize_sequence(out,float(n))
```

`make_gaussians(n, centers, sigmas)`:
```
def make_gaussians(n, centers, sigmas):
    n=int(n)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.zeros(n)
    for c,s in zip(centers,sigmas):
        s=max(float(s),1e-4)
        out += np.exp(-0.5*((x-c)/s)**2)
    out += 1e-8
    return normalize_sequence(out,float(n))
```
If centers length0 returns zeros+1e-8 uniform. okay.

`make_power_window(n,beta)`:
```
def make_power_window(n,beta):
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   base=x*(1.0-x)
   out=np.empty(n)
   with np.errstate(...): out=base**beta
   out[0]=1e-8; out[-1]=1e-8
   out +=1e-8
   return normalize_sequence(out,n)
```
If beta=0, base**0 =1; okay. Use clip.

`make_harmonic_sequence(n,rng,max_freq=6,max_amp=2.0)`:
```
def make_harmonic_sequence(n,rng,max_freq=6,max_amp=2.0):
  n=int(n)
  if n<2: return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n),n)
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  z=np.zeros(n)
  for m in range(1,max_freq+1):
    amp=rng.uniform(-max_amp,max_amp)/(m+1.0)
    ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
    z += amp*np.cos(2π m x+ph)
  amp=rng.uniform(-max_amp,max_amp)
  ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
  z += amp*np.cos(np.pi*(2*x-1)+ph)? maybe.
  out=np.exp(z)
  return normalize_sequence(out,n)
```
Good.

`add_start`:

```
def add_start(starts, arr, n, N=None):
    arr = np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    if arr.size != n:
       arr = upsample_sequence(arr,n)
    a = normalize_sequence(arr,float(n))
    try:
       if N is None: N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
       c=convolution_nt(a,N)
       s=float(a.sum())
       sc=score_from_c(c,n,s)
    except Exception:
       return
    if math.isfinite(sc):
       starts.append((sc,a.copy()))
```

```
def generate_starts(n,parent,pool,rng,max_random=10,max_starts=70):
   n=int(n); starts=[]
   N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   add_start(starts,np.ones(n),n,N)
   if parent is not None and np.asarray(parent).size>0:
      add_start(starts,upsample_sequence(parent,n),n,N)
   # pool top 4
   for i in range(min(4,len(pool))):
       pseq=pool[i][1]
       if pseq is not None and np.asarray(pseq).size>0:
           add_start(starts,upsample_sequence(pseq,n),n,N)
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   # shapes...
   ...
   starts.sort(key=_pair_score)
   if len(starts)>max_starts: starts=starts[:max_starts]
   return starts
```

`add_candidate_pool`:
```
def add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, score):
    if seq is None: return
    try: sc=float(score)
    except: return
    if not math.isfinite(sc): return
    seq=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    if seq.size==0: return
    pool.append((sc,seq.copy()))
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if len(pool)>16: del pool[-1]
```

`soft_power_search`:
- `N` optional? In local_search, pass N for efficiency. But soft_power_search can compute. Add parameter `N=None`.
- `budget_s` float.
- `p_start=4.0, p_end=128.0`.
- `target` not pass; float(n).
- Use `floor=1e-9` maybe. For large n, floor small.

Code:
```
def soft_power_search(a0,budget_s,seed=0,N=None,p_start=4.0,p_end=128.0):
   a0=np.asarray(a0,dtype=np.float64).flatten()
   n=int(a0.size)
   if n==0: return a0, inf
   target=float(n)
   if N is None: N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(0.0,float(budget_s))
   a=positive_normalize(a0,target,1e-9)
   c=convolution_nt(a,N)
   s=float(a.sum())
   score=score_from_c(c,n,s)
   best=a.copy(); best_score=score
   eta=0.12; eta_min=0.005; eta_max=1.0
   noise=0.02; accept_rel=0.02
   phase=0.0; anneal_iters=700.0
   no_improve=0; restarts=0; it=0
   phase_step=1.0/anneal_iters
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      it +=1
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or s <=0:
         a=best.copy(); c=convolution_nt(a,N); s=float(a.sum()); score=best_score
      p = p_start * (p_end/p_start)**phase
      M = float(np.max(c))
      if M <= 1e-300:
         break
      w = power_weights(c,p)
      grad = weighted_gradient_nt(a,w,N)
      g = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
      scale = float(np.std(g))
      if scale < 1e-12:
         no_improve +=1
         if no_improve > 80:
             break
         # small random kick
       else:
          g /= scale
          logf = -eta*g
          if noise > 1e-5:
             logf += rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
          logf = np.clip(logf, -2.5, 2.5)
          cand = a * np.exp(logf)
          cand = positive_normalize(cand, target, 1e-9)
          c2 = convolution_nt(cand, N)
          s2 = float(cand.sum())
          sc2 = score_from_c(c2,n,s2)
          if not math.isfinite(sc2):
              eta=max(eta_min, eta*0.7)
              continue
          improved_best = sc2 < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12) - 1e-14
          if improved_best:
             best=cand.copy(); best_score=sc2; no_improve=0; restarts=0
             eta=min(eta_max, eta*1.08)
             accept_rel=max(0.001, accept_rel*0.997)
             noise=max(0.002, noise*0.997)
          if sc2 <= score * (1.0 + accept_rel) + 1e-14:
             a=cand; c=c2; s=s2; score=sc2
             # if improved current but not best, maybe still reduce noise
             if not improved_best:
                # no change eta
                pass
          else:
             eta=max(eta_min, eta*0.75)
             no_improve += 1
          phase += phase_step
          if phase > 1.0: phase=1.0
      # stagnation
      if no_improve > 350:
          strength=0.25 + 0.08*min(restarts,5)
          cand=smooth_perturb(best,rng,strength,target)
          c=convolution_nt(cand,N); s=float(cand.sum()); sc=score_from_c(c,n,s)
          if math.isfinite(sc):
             a=cand; score=sc
          else:
             a=best.copy(); c=convolution_nt(a,N); s=float(a.sum()); score=best_score
          eta=0.25; accept_rel=0.05; noise=0.06; phase=max(0.0, phase-0.35); no_improve=0; restarts+=1
          if restarts > 10:
             # reduce aggressiveness, continue until budget
             pass
      if it % 1000 == 0:
          print(f"[soft] n={n} it={it} best={best_score:.10f} curr={score:.10f}", flush=True)
   return best,best_score
```
Need update `phase` if gradient flat without candidate? We can update in flat branch too. Put `phase += phase_step` at end of loop. Good.

If no_improve>350 and restart, we set no_improve=0 but still loop. At end `phase += phase_step`, could undo restart phase decrease. Better update phase only on accepted processing. Or after; not critical. We can structure:
```
# near end: phase += step; if phase>1 phase=1
```
Restart decreases phase then increments small 0.001; okay.

If score current becomes inf? We reset.

`lp_refine`:
```
def lp_refine(f,budget_s,seed=0,N=None,max_solves=200):
    f=np.asarray...
    n=f.size; target=n
    if N is None: N=...
    t0=... deadline=t0+budget
    f=positive_normalize(f,target)
    c=convolution_nt(f,N); s=f.sum(); score=...
    best=f; best_score=score
    blend=0.25; stale=0; solves=0
    while time<deadline and solves<max_solves:
       remain=deadline-time
       if remain < 5: break
       g = lp_minconv_direction(best, max(3.0,min(20.0,remain*0.3)), N)
       if g is None: break
       diff = float(np.max(np.abs(g-best))) if sizes same else inf
       if diff < 1e-5*max(1.0, target): break
       improved=False
       for b in (blend, 0.5*blend,0.25*blend,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.005):
          if b<=0 or b>=1: continue
          cand=(1-b)*best + b*g
          cand=positive_normalize(cand,target,1e-9)
          c2=convolution_nt(cand,N)
          sc=score_from_c(c2,n,float(cand.sum()))
          if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
              best=cand; best_score=sc; improved=True; break
       if improved:
          blend=min(0.6, blend*1.15); stale=0
          print...
       else:
          blend=max(0.015, blend*0.7); stale+=1
          if stale>6: break
       solves+=1
    return best,best_score
```
Use `_accumulate`? no.

`lp_minconv_direction` implementation details with top-level `_build_lp_dense`, `_build_lp_sparse`.

```
def _build_lp_A_dense(n,f):
   L=2*n-1
   Afull=np.zeros((L,n+1),dtype=np.float64)
   Afull[:,-1] = -1.0
   idx=np.arange(n)
   for d in range(n):
       Afull[d+idx, idx] = f[d]
   return Afull

def _build_lp_A_sparse(n,f):
   from scipy import sparse
   L=2*n-1
   diag_data=np.repeat(f[:,None], n, axis=1)
   offsets=np.arange(n,dtype=np.int64)
   A = sparse.dia_matrix((diag_data, offsets), shape=(L,n), dtype=np.float64).tocsr()
   tcol = -np.ones((L,1), dtype=np.float64)
   tcol_s = sparse.csr_matrix(tcol)
   Afull = sparse.lstack([A,tcol_s]).tocsr()
   return Afull
```
But `np.repeat(f[:,None], n, axis=1)`: if f shape (n,), f[:,None] (n,1), repeat each element n times along axis1 -> (n,n) row d all f[d]. Good. Could memory. For f length 0 no.

`lp_minconv_direction`:
```
def lp_minconv_direction(f, time_limit=10.0, N=None):
    f=np.asarray(f,dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    n=f.size
    if n < 2 or n > MAX_LP_N: return None
    c=convolution_nt(f)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if M <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(M): return None
    try:
       from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except Exception:
       return None
    # Choose build
    Afull=None
    if n <= DENSE_LP_N:
       try: Afull=_build_lp_A_dense(n,f)
       except Exception: Afull=None
    if Afull is None:
       if n > SPARSE_MAX_LP_N: return None
       try: Afull=_build_lp_A_sparse(n,f)
       except Exception:
          if n <= 1536:
             try: return _build dense? 
          return None
    c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1.0
    Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
    b_eq=np.array([float(n)])
    bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n + [(0.0,M)]
    try:
       res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=Afull,b_ub=np.zeros(2*n-1),A_eq=Aeq,b_eq=b_eq,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'time_limit':max(1.0,float(time_limit))})
    except Exception:
       try: res=linprog(... method='highs')
       except: return None
    if res is None or not hasattr(res,'x') or res.x is None: return None
    x=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != n+1: return None
    g=x[:n]; t=x[-1]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)) or not np.isfinite(t): return None
    g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
    sumg=float(g.sum())
    if sumg <=0: return None
    # scale if sum slightly high
    if sumg > float(n):
       factor=float(n)/sumg
       if factor < 1.0:
          g = g*factor
       else:
          return None
    else:
       if abs(sumg - float(n)) > max(1e-4*float(n),1e-8):
          # small deficit; if close scale up if not likely violate? try but check
          if sumg > 0.999*float(n):
              g = g*(float(n)/sumg)
          else:
              return None
    # cross check
    if N is None: N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fg=cross_convolution_nt(f,g,N)
    maxfg=float(np.max(fg))
    if not math.isfinite(maxfg): return None
    if maxfg > M*(1.0+1e-6) + 1e-9:
       # if slightly high, scale down if sum still >= n? Scaling down makes sum <n; can skip.
       if maxfg <= M*1.000001 + 1e-7 and sumg >= float(n):
          # maybe already sum n, cannot scale
          pass
       else:
          return None
    return g, maxfg
```
But if sumg < n and we scale up, maxfg may increase; cross check catches. Good.

```
def local_search(a0,budget_s,seed=0,use_lp=True,lp_frac=0.35,N=None):
   a0=...; n=a0.size; target=n
   if N is None: N=...
   t0=time.monotonic()
   c=convolution_nt(a0,N); s=sum; score=score...
   best=a0 normalized; best_score=score
   if use_lp and n <= SPARSE_MAX_LP_N:
      lp_budget = min(budget_s*lp_frac, max(15.0, 400.0*(512.0/float(n))))
      lp_budget = min(lp_budget, max(0.0, budget_s - 10.0))
      if lp_budget > 8.0:
          try:
             seq,sc=lp_refine(a0,lp_budget,seed, N)
             if sc<best_score: best=seq; best_score=sc
          except Exception: pass
   remain = budget_s - (time.monotonic()-t0)
   if remain > 3.0:
      try:
         seq,sc=soft_power_search(best,remain,seed,N)
         if sc<best_score: best=seq; best_score=sc
      except Exception: pass
   return best,best_score
```

```
def run_level(n,budget_s,parent,pool,seed):

    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

       seq=np.ones(n); return seq, fast_score_array(seq)
    # local best from starts
    best_seq=starts[0][1].copy(); best_score=starts[0][0]
    # choose k

       remain = budget_s - (time.monotonic()-t0)

       b = remain * weights[i]/total_w * (sum(weights[:i+1])? wait)
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))^2
under the exact evaluator's validity rules.

Algorithm:
- FFT-based fast convolution, score, and weighted-gradient evaluation.
- Coarse-to-fine lengths: 512 -> 1024 -> 2048 -> 4096 -> 8192 -> 16384.
- Strong starts: parent, Gaussian/double-Gaussian, cosine/power windows,
  random low-frequency shapes, and upsampled candidates from a small pool.
- Small-to-medium lengths use a convex LP step:
      given f, minimize t subject to f*g <= t, sum(g)=n, g>=0,
  then line-search a convex blend f <- (1-lambda) f + lambda g.
- All lengths use a projected multiplicative gradient method on a lifted
  power / soft-max objective, with p-annealing, adaptive step size, noise,
  and smooth restarts.
- Final candidates are validated with the exact evaluator and the best
  exact candidate is returned.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


_LP_DENSE_PRIMARY = 1024
_LP_MAX_N = 2048


def _pair_score(pair):
    return pair[0]


def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sequence(a, target_sum, max_value=1000.0):
    """Clip to valid range and scale to a target sum when possible."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = a.copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, float(max_value))
    target_sum = float(target_sum)

    for _ in range(4):
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            if a.size > 0:
                a.fill(target_sum / float(a.size))
            return a
        a *= target_sum / s
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, float(max_value))

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        if a.size > 0:
            a.fill(target_sum / float(a.size))
    else:
        a *= target_sum / s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, float(max_value))
    return a


def positive_normalize(a, target_sum, floor=1e-9):
    """Normalize and keep a tiny positive floor for log/multiplicative updates."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a, target_sum)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    floor = float(floor)
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.where(a > floor, a, floor)
        a = normalize_sequence(a, target_sum)
    return a


def convolution_nt(a, N=None):
    """FFT linear convolution a*a (full), with small negatives clipped."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None or N < L:
        N = _next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def cross_convolution_nt(a, b, N=None):
    """FFT linear convolution a*b (full), with small negatives clipped."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(b.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = n + m - 1
    if N is None or N < L:
        N = _next_pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def score_from_c(c, n, s):
    """Objective from a full convolution array c, length n and sum s."""
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(s)
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * mx / (s * s))


def fast_score_array(a, N=None):
    """Fast FFT analogue of evaluate_sequence()."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    c = convolution_nt(a, N)
    return score_from_c(c, n, s)


def weighted_gradient_nt(a, w, N):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k (a*a)_k with respect to a.
    Returns 2 * [conv(a[::-1], w)_{n-1:2n-1}].
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if N is None or N < 2 * n - 1:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    ra = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    rw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(ra * rw, N)
    return 2.0 * conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def power_weights(c, p):
    """
    Normalized power weights w_k proportional to c_k^p.
    This is the link-weight for log(sum c_k^p), approaching max as p grows.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(m):
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= float(L)
        return w
    if p < 2.0:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= float(L)
        return w

    z = c / m
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        w = z ** p
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    sw = float(w.sum())
    amax = int(np.argmax(c))
    if sw <= 1e-300:
        w.fill(0.0)
        w[amax] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= sw
        if w[amax] <= 0.0:
            w[amax] = 1.0
            w *= 0.5
    return w


def upsample_sequence(seq, n):
    """Linearly resample a sequence to length n and normalize to sum n."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return seq
    if seq.size == 0:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), float(n))
    if seq.size == n:
        return normalize_sequence(seq, float(n))

    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(xnew, xold, seq)
    return normalize_sequence(out, float(n))


def smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength, target_sum):
    """Smooth multiplicative perturbation used for restarts."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if n > 2:
        for m in range(1, 5):
            amp = float(strength) / float(m + 1)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
            z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * x + ph)

    if n > 16 and rng.random() < 0.65:
        center = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)
        width = rng.uniform(0.02, 0.18)
        amp = rng.normal(0.0, float(strength))
        z += amp * np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - center) / width) ** 2)

    cand = a * np.exp(z)
    return positive_normalize(cand, target_sum, 1e-9)


def make_gaussians(n, centers, sigmas):
    """Sum of normalized Gaussians on [0,1]."""
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for c, s in zip(centers, sigmas):
        s = max(float(s), 1e-4)
        out += np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - float(c)) / s) ** 2)
    out += 1e-8
    return normalize_sequence(out, float(n))


def make_power_window(n, beta):
    """Endpoints-zero power window, x^beta(1-x)^beta, with a tiny floor."""
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    base = x * (1.0 - x)
    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore", divide="ignore", over="ignore"):
        out = base ** float(beta)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out[0] = 1e-8
    out[-1] = 1e-8
    out += 1e-8
    return normalize_sequence(out, float(n))


def make_harmonic_sequence(n, rng, max_freq=6, max_amp=2.0):
    """Random low-frequency cosine shape, exponentiated to be positive."""
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), float(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, int(max_freq) + 1):
        amp = rng.uniform(-float(max_amp), float(max_amp)) / float(m + 1)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * x + ph)
    amp = rng.uniform(-float(max_amp), float(max_amp))
    ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
    z += amp * np.cos(math.pi * (2.0 * x - 1.0) + ph)
    out = np.exp(z)
    return normalize_sequence(out, float(n))


def add_start(starts, arr, n, N=None):
    """Score and add a candidate start if finite."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
        n = int(n)
        if arr.size != n:
            arr = upsample_sequence(arr, n)
        a = normalize_sequence(arr, float(n))
        if N is None:
            N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        c = convolution_nt(a, N)
        s = float(a.sum())
        sc = score_from_c(c, n, s)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            starts.append((sc, a.copy()))
    except Exception:
        pass


def generate_starts(n, parent, pool, rng, max_random=8, max_starts=80):
    """Create a diverse scored pool of starts for length n."""
    n = int(n)
    starts = []
    N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    add_start(starts, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n, N)

    if parent is not None:
        parr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
        if parr.size > 0:
            add_start(starts, upsample_sequence(parr, n), n, N)

    for i in range(min(4, len(pool))):
        try:
            pseq = pool[i][1]
            parr = np.asarray(pseq, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
            if parr.size > 0:
                add_start(starts, upsample_sequence(parr, n), n, N)
        except Exception:
            pass

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    # Symmetric and asymmetric single Gaussians.
    for s in (0.12, 0.20, 0.30, 0.45, 0.65, 0.90, 1.30):
        add_start(starts, make_gaussians(n, [0.5], [s]), n, N)

    for center in (0.15, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 0.85):
        for s in (0.18, 0.32, 0.50, 0.80):
            add_start(starts, make_gaussians(n, [center], [s]), n, N)

    # Two-lobe Gaussians.
    for d in (0.0, 0.08, 0.15, 0.25, 0.35):
        for s in (0.18, 0.30, 0.45, 0.65, 1.00):
            centers = [0.5 - d, 0.5 + d]
            if d == 0.0:
                add_start(starts, make_gaussians(n, [0.5], [s]), n, N)
            else:
                add_start(starts, make_gaussians(n, centers, [s, s]), n, N)

    # Exponential quadratic windows: edge-peaked or center-peaked.
    q = (2.0 * t - 1.0) ** 2
    for A in (-2.0, -1.2, -0.6, 0.0, 0.6, 1.2, 2.0):
        add_start(starts, np.exp(A * q), n, N)

    # Power windows.
    for beta in (0.25, 0.50, 1.00, 2.00, 4.00):
        add_start(starts, make_power_window(n, beta), n, N)

    # Polynomial edge/center shapes.
    for A in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.0, 0.4, 0.8, 1.2):
        out = np.maximum(1.0 + A * q, 1e-7)
        add_start(starts, out, n, N)

    # Cosine exponentials.
    for A in (0.5, 1.0, 1.8):
        add_start(starts, np.exp(A * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * t)), n, N)
        add_start(starts, np.exp(A * np.cos(4.0 * math.pi * t)), n, N)
        add_start(starts, np.exp(-A * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * t)), n, N)

    # A few randomized smooth starts.
    for _ in range(int(max_random)):
        add_start(starts, make_harmonic_sequence(n, rng, max_freq=6, max_amp=2.0), n, N)

    starts.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if len(starts) > int(max_starts):
        starts = starts[:int(max_starts)]
    return starts


def add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, score):
    """Keep a small pool of the best candidates found so far."""
    if seq is None:
        return
    try:
        sc = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return
    try:
        seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    except Exception:
        return
    if seq.size == 0:
        return
    pool.append((sc, seq.copy()))
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if len(pool) > 16:
        del pool[-1]


def _build_lp_A_dense(n, f):
    """Dense [A, -1] for the LP: A g - t <= 0."""
    L = 2 * n - 1
    Afull = np.zeros((L, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    Afull[:, -1] = -1.0
    idx = np.arange(n)
    for d in range(n):
        Afull[d + idx, idx] = f[d]
    return Afull


def _build_lp_A_sparse(n, f):
    """Sparse [A, -1] using a banded Toeplitz structure."""
    from scipy import sparse

    L = 2 * n - 1
    # Diagonal d (row - col = d) has constant value f[d] for all n columns.
    diag_data = np.repeat(f[:, None], n, axis=1)
    offsets = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    A = sparse.dia_matrix((diag_data, offsets), shape=(L, n), dtype=np.float64).tocsr()
    tcol = -np.ones((L, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    tcol_s = sparse.csr_matrix(tcol)
    Afull = sparse.lstack([A, tcol_s]).tocsr()
    return Afull


def lp_minconv_direction(f, time_limit=10.0, N=None):
    """
    Solve min t s.t. f*g <= t, sum(g)=n, g>=0.
    Returns g (feasible, sum n) or None.
    """
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    n = int(f.size)
    if n < 2 or n > _LP_MAX_N:
        return None

    c = convolution_nt(f, N)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if M <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(M):
        return None

    try:
        from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except Exception:
        return None

    Afull = None
    if n <= _LP_DENSE_PRIMARY:
        try:
            Afull = _build_lp_A_dense(n, f)
        except Exception:
            Afull = None

    if Afull is None:
        if n <= _LP_MAX_N:
            try:
                Afull = _build_lp_A_sparse(n, f)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    Afull = _build_lp_A_dense(n, f)
                except Exception:
                    Afull = None

    if Afull is None:
        return None

    L = 2 * n - 1
    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[-1] = 1.0
    Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.array([float(n)], dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(0.0, float(M))]

    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=Afull,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=Aeq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": max(1.0, float(time_limit))},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=Afull,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=Aeq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x") or res.x is None:
        return None

    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != n + 1:
        return None

    g = x[:n]
    t = x[-1]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)) or not np.isfinite(t):
        return None

    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    sumg = float(g.sum())
    if sumg <= 1e-15:
        return None

    # Repair tiny equality violations.  Scaling up is risky, scaling down safe.
    if sumg > float(n):
        factor = float(n) / sumg
        g = g * factor
    elif sumg < float(n):
        if sumg >= 0.999 * float(n):
            g = g * (float(n) / sumg)
        else:
            return None

    # Verify feasibility and sum after repair.
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fg = cross_convolution_nt(f, g, N)
    maxfg = float(np.max(fg))
    if not math.isfinite(maxfg):
        return None
    if maxfg > float(M) * (1.0 + 1e-6) + 1e-9:
        return None

    sumg = float(g.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(sumg) or sumg <= 0.0:
        return None
    if abs(sumg - float(n)) > max(1e-3 * float(n), 1e-8):
        return None

    return g, maxfg


def lp_refine(f, budget_s, seed=0, N=None, max_solves=200):
    """Repeated LP-direction line-search blending for small/medium n."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    n = int(f.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return f, float("inf")

    target = float(n)
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    f = positive_normalize(f, target, 1e-9)
    c = convolution_nt(f, N)
    s = float(f.sum())
    score = score_from_c(c, n, s)

    best = f.copy()
    best_score = score

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    blend = 0.25
    stale = 0
    solves = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and solves < int(max_solves):
        remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remain < 5.0:
            break

        tl = max(2.0, min(20.0, 0.30 * remain))
        try:
            out = lp_minconv_direction(best, tl, N)
        except Exception:
            out = None

        if out is None:
            break

        g, t_opt = out
        if g.size != n:
            break

        diff = float(np.max(np.abs(g - best)))
        if diff < 1e-6 * max(1.0, target):
            break

        improved = False
        for b in (blend, 0.5 * blend, 0.25 * blend, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015):
            b = float(b)
            if b <= 1e-12 or b >= 1.0:
                continue
            cand = (1.0 - b) * best + b * g
            cand = positive_normalize(cand, target, 1e-9)
            c2 = convolution_nt(cand, N)
            sc = score_from_c(c2, n, float(cand.sum()))
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = cand.copy()
                best_score = sc
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            blend = min(0.60, blend * 1.15)
            stale = 0
            print(f"[LP] n={n} solve={solves} best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)
        else:
            blend = max(0.015, blend * 0.70)
            stale += 1
            if stale > 6:
                break

        solves += 1

    return best, best_score


def soft_power_search(a0, budget_s, seed=0, N=None, p_start=4.0, p_end=128.0):
    """
    Multiplicative projected-gradient search.
    The direction is the gradient of log(sum (c/M)^p), annealed in p.
    """
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    n = int(a0.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return a0, float("inf")

    target = float(n)
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    a = positive_normalize(a0, target, 1e-9)
    c = convolution_nt(a, N)
    s = float(a.sum())
    score = score_from_c(c, n, s)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    eta = 0.12
    eta_min = 0.005
    eta_max = 1.0
    noise = 0.02
    accept_rel = 0.02

    phase = 0.0
    phase_step = 1.0 / 700.0
    no_improve = 0
    restarts = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or s <= 1e-15:
            a = best.copy()
            c = convolution_nt(a, N)
            s = float(a.sum())
            score = best_score

        p = float(p_start) * (float(p_end) / float(p_start)) ** phase
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(M):
            break

        try:
            w = power_weights(c, p)
            grad = weighted_gradient_nt(a, w, N)
            g = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
            scale = float(np.std(g))
        except Exception:
            break

        if scale > 1e-12:
            g /= scale
            logf = -eta * g
            if noise > 1e-5:
                logf += rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
            logf = np.clip(logf, -2.5, 2.5)
            cand = a * np.exp(logf)
            cand = positive_normalize(cand, target, 1e-9)
            c2 = convolution_nt(cand, N)
            s2 = float(cand.sum())
            sc2 = score_from_c(c2, n, s2)

            if not math.isfinite(sc2):
                eta = max(eta_min, eta * 0.7)
                no_improve += 1
            else:
                improved_best = sc2 < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12) - 1e-14
                if improved_best:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_score = sc2
                    no_improve = 0
                    restarts = 0
                    eta = min(eta_max, eta * 1.08)
                    accept_rel = max(0.001, accept_rel * 0.997)
                    noise = max(0.002, noise * 0.997)

                if sc2 <= score * (1.0 + accept_rel) + 1e-14:
                    a = cand
                    c = c2
                    s = s2
                    score = sc2
                else:
                    eta = max(eta_min, eta * 0.75)
                    no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 80:
                break

        phase += phase_step
        if phase > 1.0:
            phase = 1.0

        if no_improve > 350:
            strength = 0.25 + 0.08 * float(min(restarts, 5))
            try:
                cand = smooth_perturb(best, rng, strength, target)
                c = convolution_nt(cand, N)
                s = float(cand.sum())
                sc = score_from_c(c, n, s)
                if math.isfinite(sc):
                    a = cand
                    score = sc
                else:
                    a = best.copy()
                    c = convolution_nt(a, N)
                    s = float(a.sum())
                    score = best_score
            except Exception:
                a = best.copy()
                c = convolution_nt(a, N)
                s = float(a.sum())
                score = best_score

            eta = 0.25
            accept_rel = 0.05
            noise = 0.06
            phase = max(0.0, phase - 0.35)
            no_improve = 0
            restarts += 1

        if it % 1000 == 0:
            print(
                f"[soft] n={n} it={it} p={p:.2f} "
                f"best={best_score:.10f} curr={score:.10f}",
                flush=True,
            )

    return best, best_score


def local_search(a0, budget_s, seed=0, use_lp=True, lp_frac=0.35, N=None):
    """LP (when useful) followed by soft-power multiplicative refinement."""
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    n = int(a0.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return a0, float("inf")

    target = float(n)
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    a0 = positive_normalize(a0, target, 1e-9)
    c0 = convolution_nt(a0, N)
    s0 = float(a0.sum())
    score0 = score_from_c(c0, n, s0)

    best = a0.copy()
    best_score = score0

    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if use_lp and n <= _LP_MAX_N:
        lp_budget = min(float(budget_s) * float(lp_frac),
                        max(12.0, 420.0 * (512.0 / float(n))))
        lp_budget = min(lp_budget, max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0))
        if lp_budget > 6.0:
            try:
                seq, sc = lp_refine(a0, lp_budget, seed=seed, N=N)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best = seq
                    best_score = sc
            except Exception:
                pass

    remain = float(budget_s) - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remain > 3.0:
        try:
            seq, sc = soft_power_search(best, remain, seed=seed, N=N,
                                        p_start=4.0, p_end=128.0)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = seq
                best_score = sc
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best, best_score


def run_level(n, budget_s, parent, pool, seed):
    """Run several local searches at one length and update the pool."""
    n = int(n)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if budget_s <= 8.0:
        if parent is not None and np.asarray(parent).size > 0:
            seq = upsample_sequence(parent, n)
        else:
            seq = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), float(n))
        return seq, fast_score_array(seq)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    try:
        starts = generate_starts(n, parent, pool, rng,
                                 max_random=6 if n > 8192 else 10,
                                 max_starts=80)
    except Exception:
        starts = []

    if not starts:
        seq = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), float(n))
        return seq, fast_score_array(seq)

    for sc, seq in starts[:5]:
        add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, sc)

    best_seq = starts[0][1].copy()
    best_score = starts[0][0]

    if n <= 512:
        k = min(5, len(starts))
    elif n <= 1024:
        k = min(4, len(starts))
    elif n <= 2048:
        k = min(3, len(starts))
    elif n <= 4096:
        k = min(3, len(starts))
    else:
        k = min(3 if budget_s > 700.0 else 2, len(starts))

    weights = [1.7, 1.1, 0.9, 0.8, 0.7][:k]
    total_w = float(sum(weights))

    for i in range(k):
        remain = budget_s - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if remain <= 5.0:
            break
        b = budget_s * float(weights[i]) / total_w
        b = min(max(5.0, b), remain - 3.0)
        if b <= 0.0:
            break

        sc, seq = starts[i]
        try:
            seq2, sc2 = local_search(
                seq,
                b,
                seed=int(seed * 13 + i),
                use_lp=(n <= _LP_MAX_N),
                lp_frac=0.35,
            )
        except Exception:
            seq2, sc2 = seq, fast_score_array(seq)

        if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
            best_seq = seq2.copy()
            best_score = sc2

        add_candidate_pool(pool, seq2, sc2)
        print(f"[level] n={n} start{i} fast={sc2:.10f} local_best={best_score:.10f}", flush=True)

    return best_seq, best_score


def _exact_eval(seq):
    """Call the provided exact evaluator safely."""
    try:
        if seq is None:
            return float("inf")
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
        if arr.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        val = evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def sanitize_for_return(seq):
    """Return a valid Python list of non-negative finite floats."""
    try:
        seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
    except Exception:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    if seq.size == 0:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    n = int(seq.size)
    s = float(seq.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    scale = float(n) / s
    mx = float(seq.max())
    if mx > 1e-300 and mx * scale > 1000.0:
        scale = 1000.0 / mx

    seq = seq * scale
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    if float(seq.sum()) < 0.01:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    return [float(x) for x in seq]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.  Searches for a valid non-negative sequence and returns
    the best exact-evaluated sequence found within the budget.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not math.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0

    parent = None
    try:
        hp = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if hp is not None:
            parr = np.asarray(hp, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
            if parr.size > 0:
                parent = normalize_sequence(parr, float(parr.size))
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if budget < 25.0:
        if parent is not None:
            return sanitize_for_return(parent)
        return sanitize_for_return(normalize_sequence(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64), 1024.0))

    eval_reserve = min(120.0, max(40.0, 0.05 * budget))
    search_budget = max(10.0, budget - eval_reserve)

    if search_budget < 250.0:
        levels = [(512, 1.00)]
    elif search_budget < 700.0:
        levels = [(512, 0.15), (1024, 0.35), (2048, 0.50)]
    elif search_budget < 1300.0:
        levels = [(512, 0.07), (1024, 0.12), (2048, 0.18), (4096, 0.63)]
    else:
        levels = [
            (512, 0.06),
            (1024, 0.09),
            (2048, 0.13),
            (4096, 0.19),
            (8192, 0.21),
            (16384, 0.32),
        ]

    pool = []

    # Seed the pool with a few simple valid candidates.
    if parent is not None:
        add_candidate_pool(pool, parent, fast_score_array(parent))
    add_candidate_pool(pool, normalize_sequence(np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64), 512.0),
                       2.0)
    add_candidate_pool(pool, make_gaussians(512, [0.5], [0.45]),
                       fast_score_array(make_gaussians(512, [0.5], [0.45])))
    add_candidate_pool(pool, make_gaussians(512, [0.35, 0.65], [0.35, 0.35]),
                       fast_score_array(make_gaussians(512, [0.35, 0.65], [0.35, 0.35])))

    global_best_seq = None
    global_best_score = float("inf")

    # If any existing parent is already very good, carry it forward.
    if parent is not None:
        ps = fast_score_array(parent)
        if math.isfinite(ps) and ps < global_best_score:
            global_best_seq = parent.copy()
            global_best_score = ps

    search_deadline = t0 + search_budget

    for idx, (n, frac) in enumerate(levels):
        if time.monotonic() >= search_deadline - 5.0:
            break
        b = search_budget * float(frac)
        b = min(b, max(0.0, search_deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0))
        if b < 12.0:
            break

        try:
            seq, sc = run_level(n, b, global_best_seq, pool, int(seed + 1000 * idx))
        except Exception:
            seq, sc = None, float("inf")

        if seq is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_best_score:
            global_best_score = sc
            global_best_seq = seq
            add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, sc)
            print(f"[global] n={seq.size} fast_best={sc:.10f}", flush=True)

    # Ensure we always have a valid fallback.
    if global_best_seq is None:
        if parent is not None:
            global_best_seq = parent.copy()
            global_best_score = fast_score_array(parent)
        else:
            global_best_seq = normalize_sequence(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64), 1024.0)
            global_best_score = fast_score_array(global_best_seq)

    add_candidate_pool(pool, global_best_seq, global_best_score)
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)

    # Exact evaluation of a few promising candidates.
    exact_best_seq = global_best_seq
    exact_best_score = float("inf")

    large_evals = 0
    total_evals = 0
    hard_eval_limit = 5
    if global_best_seq is not None and global_best_seq.size > 8192:
        hard_eval_limit = 3

    # Evaluate current global best first (it may not appear first due to exact/FFT differences).
    if global_best_seq is not None and global_best_seq.size <= 18000:
        ev = _exact_eval(global_best_seq)
        if math.isfinite(ev):
            exact_best_score = ev
            exact_best_seq = global_best_seq.copy()
        total_evals += 1
        if global_best_seq.size > 8192:
            large_evals += 1
        print(f"[exact] global_best exact={ev:.10f}", flush=True)

    for sc, seq in pool[:12]:
        if total_evals >= hard_eval_limit:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > t0 + budget - 12.0:
            break

        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).flatten()
        if arr.size == 0:
            continue
        if arr.size > 18000:
            continue
        if arr.size > 8192 and large_evals >= 1:
            continue

        ev = _exact_eval(arr)
        total_evals += 1
        if arr.size > 8192:
            large_evals += 1

        if math.isfinite(ev):
            print(f"[exact] n={arr.size} fast={sc:.10f} exact={ev:.10f}", flush=True)
            if ev < exact_best_score:
                exact_best_score = ev
                exact_best_seq = arr.copy()

    if exact_best_seq is None:
        exact_best_seq = global_best_seq
    if exact_best_seq is None:
        exact_best_seq = normalize_sequence(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64), 1024.0)

    return sanitize_for_return(exact_best_seq)
```
